# B Correction.1 — exact pre-acquisition descriptor-to-admission contract

NORMATIVE_DESIGN_ONLY; no executable authorization or runtime PASS. The A dependency is its independently APPROVED Correction.2 exact design/hash/report in ACCEPTED-A-DEPENDENCY.json. A-D1-01 R1 provenance stays CLOSED and A-D1-02/A-C1-01 stays resolved. A editorial P2 A-C2-01 remains disclosed/nonblocking with immutable accepted bytes under actual human continuation approval. Neither A's two rejections nor B's original rejection is rewritten or inherited as B acceptance.

## 1. Closed scalar interface and trusted inputs

The pure static operation is SharedSchemaCompatibilityV1.evaluateRequiredSlices. Its semantic input tuple is (InstalledDescriptorV1 actual, TrustedExpectationsV1 expectedCurrent50, LIST<MigrationExpectationV1> expectedNominal49), using exactly the existing ordered accepted common scalar schemas embedded in ACCEPTED-A-SCALAR-SCHEMAS.json. All values are immutable defensive scalar copies; no resource/context/token/transaction/configuration object, lambda validator, caller-selected policy or Boolean SUCCESS is accepted. Public scalar construction is permitted only as data and cannot authenticate a physical owner. Concrete Java carriers must consume the immutable canonical A API under the later actual-A-output executable gate; no new file/type dependency outside the frozen paths is permitted.

Actual descriptor input comes ONLY from the current private common owner capture on its registered RR resource and complete owner-initial plan. expectedCurrent50 and expectedNominal49 originate in competent independent fixture/preflight control-plane validation of the protected current migration bytes and complete required definitions, installed privately before material SQL. No input expectation is derived from accepting current observations as their own expectation, arbitrary external request or runtime retry. The current50 expected requiredGroups contain R3/R4 definitions derived independently from the closed V46 nominal and current V47 adjustment slices, under pinned compatible provider/driver metadata and SELECT-only role requirements. Full-schema expected fingerprint describes actual current50; it is not the old49 fingerprint.

The helper is pure: no SQL, JDBC/JPA/Spring runtime/capability/owner access, testinfra import, metadata acquisition or schema repair. It validates the complete scalar relationship and returns existing common SliceDecisionDataV1 data only. It issues no token and never marks private completion.

## 2. Required deterministic comparison

1. Validate exact common ordered record shapes, complete type/null/absence/value constraints and canonical collections using accepted A's encoder/invariants.     "function": "Reject null row list; filter null rows; take nonnull turnId; distinct; Java UUID.compareTo SIGNED natural order. The original method and executor are reused. If empty, OMIT the assignment SELECT and record exact conditional resolution, never bind an empty IN list. If nonempty, resolve expected assignment binds from these actual decoded member rows BEFORE assignment SQL; precommitted rule, not observation-defined arbitrary expected binds.",
    "ordering": "R2 salon SQL binds also signed natural; R2 canonical scope uses original unsigned UUID order. R1 salon scope/query uses original unsigned order. No global UUID sorting override."
